## `AGO-2740-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Integrating-Sphere Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Luminaires Goniophotometer Luminous Flux & CRI Spectrum
- **Intro:** Integrating-Sphere Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Luminaires Goniophotometer Luminous Flux & CRI Spectrum — Pre-calibration Environmental Verification: Temperature: 25±1°C; Relative Humidity: 50±5% RH; Vibration: ≤0.1 m/s²; Ambient illuminance: <1 lux at sphere surface.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 25±1°C; Relative Humidity: 50±5% RH; Vibration: ≤0.1 m/s²; Ambient illuminance: <1 lux at sphere surface.
- **Standards:** `ISO/CIE 13078:2018`, `IEC 62612:2018`, `CIE 13.3:1995`, `ISO 10012:2003`

## `ARE-2740-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ARE` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Eutectic-Die-Attach Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Power LED Chip-on-Board Die Bonder & Wire Bonder
- **Intro:** Eutectic-Die-Attach Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for High-Power LED Chip-on-Board Die Bonder & Wire Bonder — Verify Cold State and Isolate Energy Sources: Machine temperature < 40°C; air pressure at 0 bar; electrical power off with zero voltage (verify with a multimeter).
